professional with more than 25  years’ experience in various

Schools Closed to Open 
Board  of  Education  adopts  a  resolution  to  close  Barnard, 
Bemis, Schroeder and Boulan Park Schools as well as Athens 
and  Troy  High  Schools  to  open  enrollment  for  the  2020‐
2021 school year, with the exception of not more than (25) 
twenty‐five incoming ninth grade students who reside in the 
Troy High School attendance area but wish to attend Athens 
High  School.    Students  with  siblings  attending  Athens  and 
Troy  High  and  currently  enrolled  in  grades  9  –  11  will  be 
allowed to open enroll to rem ain for the 2020‐2021 school 
year.

Dear Kris: 
Please find below a summary of furniture selections and dollar estimates associated with the Media 2.0 
adoption program furnishings effort.  In order to provide equity and consistency across the district, a set of 
“kits” was developed, designed around activity patterns. Each elementary school was asked to select their 
desired combination of kits up to $80,000 with any additional selections covered by that school’s PTO 
funds.  There is one deviation from this rule; Martell Elementary will receive a complete furniture 
replacement due to a flood. The kits developed are as follows:

8. BUSINESS  
 B.  CONSIDERATION:  PROFESSIONAL AUDIT SERVICES 
 Responses to a Request for Proposal for Professional Audit Services 
were reviewed  and interviews conducted by Rick West, Assistant 
Superintendent, Business Services ; Kandice Moynihan , Finance 
Director, and the Business Department  staff, and it is recommended 
the bid be awarded as stated below.    
 RECOMMENDED ACTION:  Accept the resolution below. 
SUGGESTED RESOLUTION 
 Moved by:   
 Supported by: 
Be it therefore resolved that professional audit services be awarded to 
Plante Moran, PLLC consistent with their submitted proposal dated February 
12, 2020  in t he amount not to exceed $ 57,500.00 for 20 19-2020, 
$58,600.00 for 20 20-2021, $59,700.00 for 2021 -2022, $60,900.00 for 
2022-2023 and $ 62,100.00 for 20 23-2024 and that these amounts be 
approved as an expenditure of the General Fund. 
 Yes: 
 No: 
120
Memorandum 
  TO: Dr. Rich Machesky, Superintendent 
   and Board of Education 
Business 
FROM: Rick West, Assistant Superintendent,  
Office 
   Business Services     
4400 Livernois 
Phone: 248.823.4022 
   Recommendation 
Fax: 248.823.4013 
DATE: February 28, 2020 
A request for proposal  was issued  for the District ’s professional audit services  in 
January and proposals were due on February 12th.  Six proposals were received (see 
attached bid tab)  and evaluated by our Finance team and me using the attached 
Evaluation tool.  Given the results of the evaluations, two  firms were selected  for 
interviews conducted on February 27th.  
The audit firms, Plante Moran and Yeo & Yeo, were interviewed by Kandice Moynihan, 
Pat Orczykowski, LaTisha Goulbourne, Josie Simon, Margaret Weaver, and me.  Both 
firms presented well and were  thoroughly considered for selection to  provide the 
services we seek from an independent audit firm.  Along with the factors in the 
evaluation tool, the committee considered  each firm ’s size, experience, audit 
approach, assigned audit team, customer service model, and cost.  Intangibles such 
as the ability to connect  with and build a partnership were also factors for 
consideration.   
As a result , the committee is excited to recommend that the proposal from  Plante 
Moran be accepted and a contract be awarded to  have them  provide annual 
professional audit services to  the District for the next five years .  We found their  
presentation and proposal to be complete, firm and audit team experienced , audit 
approach aligned with our expectations, and a superior customer service model that 
meets our needs .  Plante Moran will perform an annual independent audit of our 
financial statements while providing a fresh perspective, collaborative partnership,  
and highly competent audit team. 
Plante Moran’s proposed cost of $57,500 for the June 30, 2020 annual audit will be a 
savings of $250 from our cost of $57,750 for the June 30, 2019 annual audit.  
Attached are copies of Plante Moran’s presentation and proposal.  Printed copies will 
be provided at the Board of Education workshop on Tuesday, March 3rd, 2020. 
If you have any questions, comments, or concerns regarding this recommendation, 
please contact me. 
121
  RE: Professional Audit Services   
Troy, MI 48098

Subject: Paging Upgrade RFP #9899 Recommendation Letter 
Beth, 
On January 27, 2020, Troy School District issued a Request for Proposal (RFP) soliciting bids for district-wide paging 
upgrades.  The RFP was posted to the District’s website.  Convergent also spoke with several vendors regarding the 
solicitation and pointed them to the website to download the RFP and supporting documentation.  On February 
18, 2020, sealed bids were accepted and opened by the District. Two proposals were received.  The initial results 
were tabulated as follows: 
Contractor Base Bid 
The DataCom Group $778,105.00 
Moss $1,114,132.00 
All proposals were evaluated for compliance with the RFP requirements, pricing, technical solution and acceptance 
of terms and conditions of the RFP. Both were compliant.   
A technical evaluation of the system was conducted with the manufacturer’s fie ld engineer and it was found the 
proposed system meets all RFP requirements. 
On February 25th the District IT staff and Convergent Technology Partners conducted a interview with the low 
bidder, The DataCom Group, where we discussed scope of work, TSD expectations and scheduling. All questions 
and topics discussed were answered satisfactory. The DataCom Group has previously worked for TSD installing 
paging systems in the ECC and IAE and are familiar with the system and the district IT staff.  
Based on these factors, we recommend the award of the Paging Upgrade (RFP #9899) project to the low qualified 
bidder, The DataCom Group, in the amount of $778,105.00, with an additional ~10% contingency of $77,810.00 
for a total of $855,915.00.  Please contact me with any questions at 810-223-0531. 
Respectfully, 
Eric Helsel, RCDD/OSP/RTPM 
Project Manager, Convergent Technology Partners
